   Location: .\backend\.venv\Lib\site-packages\openai\auth\_workload.py:47:0
46	
47	def k8s_service_account_token_provider(
48	    token_file_path: str | Path = "/var/run/secrets/kubernetes.io/serviceaccount/token",
49	) -> SubjectTokenProvider:
50	    """
51	    Get a subject token provider for Kubernetes clusters with Workload Identity configured.
52	
53	    Cloud providers typically mount the subject token as a file in the container.
54	
55	    Args:
56	        token_file_path: path to the mounted service account token file. Defaults to `/var/run/secrets/kubernetes.io/serviceaccount/token`.
57	    """
58	
59	    def get_token() -> str:
60	        try:
61	            with open(token_file_path, "r") as f:
62	                token = f.read().strip()
63	                if not token:
64	                    raise SubjectTokenProviderError(f"The token file at {token_file_path} is empty.")
65	                return token
66	        except Exception as e:
67	            raise SubjectTokenProviderError(f"Failed to read the token file at {token_file_path}: {e}") from e
68	
69	    return {"token_type": "jwt", "get_token": get_token}
70
